Returns (modified_paragraph_ids, evidence_log).\"\"\"\n\n    evidence: list[str] = []\n    modified: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()  # (xml_path, p_id)\n\n    start = update[\"window_start\"]\n    end = update[\"window_end\"]\n    new_window = f\"{start} ~ {end}\"\n\n    # Helper: mark paragraph modified and remove layout cache\n    def mark_modified(xml_path: Path, p: ET.Element) -> None:\n        pid = p.get(\"id\", \"\")\n        modified.add((str(xml_path), pid))\n\n    # 1) Update all paragraphs based on semantic labels / known patterns.\n    for xml_path, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        root = tree.getroot()\n        for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n            old_txt = p_text(p)\n\n            if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in old_txt:\n                continue\n\n            new_txt = old_txt\n\n            if \"고객사:\" in old_txt:\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"고객사:\\s*.*$\", f\"고객사: {update['customer']}\", old_txt)\n            if \"현 담당자:\" in old_txt:\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"현 담당자:\\s*.*$\", f\"현 담당자: {update['owner_name']}\", new_txt)\n            if \"가격 메모:\" in old_txt:\n                # Keep the label and replace the tail.\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"가격 메모:\\s*.*$\", f\"가격 메모: {update['note']}\", new_txt)\n\n            if \"갱신 윈도우 확인:\" in old_txt:\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"갱신 윈도우 확인:\\s*.*$\", f\"갱신 윈도우 확인: {new_window}\", new_txt)\n            elif \"갱신 윈도우:\" in old_txt:\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"갱신 윈도우:\\s*.*$\", f\"갱신 윈도우: {new_window}\", new_txt)\n\n            if old_txt.strip() == \"Standard-18M\":\n                new_txt = update[\"band\"]\n            if old_txt.strip() == \"ops-old@northwind.example\":\n                new_txt = update[\"escalation_email\"]\n\n            # If changed, rewrite and remove layout cache\n            if new_txt != old_txt:\n                rewrite_paragraph_to_single_run(p, new_txt)\n                removed = remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p)\n                mark_modified(xml_path, p)\n                evidence.append(f\"Updated {xml_path.name} p@id={p.get('id')} : '{old_txt}' -> '{new_txt}' (removed layout={removed})\")\n\n    # 2) Replace follow-up paragraphs (the three old follow-up lines).\n    # Find the file containing them; in this task it is section1.xml.\n    followup_pat = re.compile(r\"^\\s*\\d+\\.\\s*old follow-up\\b\", re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    followup_file: Path | None = None\n    followup_ps: list[ET.Element] = []\n    followup_parent: ET.Element | None = None\n\n    for xml_path, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        root = tree.getroot()\n        for parent in root.iter():\n            # Need ordered child list, so we scan each parent for <hp:p> children.\n            children = list(parent)\n            ps = [c for c in children if c.tag == f\"{{{HP_NS}}}p\"]\n            if not ps:\n                continue\n            matches = [p for p in ps if followup_pat.match(p_text(p))]\n            if len(matches) == 3:\n                followup_file = xml_path\n                followup_ps = matches\n                followup_parent = parent\n                break\n        if followup_file:\n            break\n\n    if not followup_file or not followup_parent or len(followup_ps) != 3:\n        raise SystemExit(\"Could not locate the 3 existing old follow-up paragraphs to replace\")\n\n    # Determine insertion index (at first match's position among parent's children)\n    parent_children = list(followup_parent)\n    first_idx = parent_children.index(followup_ps[0])\n\n    # Capture template paragraph style from first old follow-up\n    template = deepcopy(followup_ps[0])\n\n    # Remove old paragraphs\n    old_ids = []\n    for p in followup_ps:\n        old_ids.append(p.get(\"id\", \"\"))\n        followup_parent.remove(p)\n        evidence.append(f\"Removed old follow-up paragraph id={p.get('id')} text='{p_text(p)}'\")\n\n    # Collect existing ids to avoid collisions if we need more than 3\n    existing_ids = set()\n    for p in followup_parent.getroottree().getroot().findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n        pid = p.get(\"id\")\n        if pid is not None:\n            existing_ids.add(pid)\n\n    # Find numeric max for id allocation\n    numeric_ids = [int(x) for x in existing_ids if x.isdigit()]\n    max_id = max(numeric_ids) if numeric_ids else 0\n\n    def alloc_id(i: int) -> str:\n        nonlocal max_id\n        if i < len(old_ids) and old_ids[i] and old_ids[i] not in existing_ids:\n            return old_ids[i]\n        # Prefer reusing removed numeric ids (safe) for the first three\n        if i < len(old_ids) and old_ids[i] and old_ids[i].isdigit():\n            return old_ids[i]\n        max_id += 1\n        while str(max_id) in existing_ids:\n            max_id += 1\n        return str(max_id)\n\n    new_items = [r[\"item\"] for r in followups]\n\n    new_paras: list[ET.Element] = []\n    for i, item in enumerate(new_items, start=1):\n        p_new = deepcopy(template)\n        # Assign id safely\n        pid = alloc_id(i - 1)\n        p_new.set(\"id\", pid)\n\n        # Set text like \"1. <item>\"\n        new_line = f\"{i}. {item}\"\n        rewrite_paragraph_to_single_run(p_new, new_line)\n\n        # Ensure any modified paragraphs have had layout cache removed (already done in edit paths).\n    return modified, evidence\n\n\ndef write_xml_trees(xml_trees: dict[Path, ET.ElementTree]) -> None:\n    for path, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        tree.write(path, encoding=\"UTF-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n\n\ndef rezip_hwpx(extracted_root: Path, out_zip: Path, original_members: list[str]) -> None:\n    # <hp:lineSegArray>) ONLY from paragraphs whose text content was modified.\n- Repackages as a .hwpx (zip) preserving original file structure.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport Path(\"/root\")\nIN_HWPX = ROOT / \"renewal_playbook.hwpx\"\nUPDATE_JSON = ROOT / \"renewal_update.json\"\nFOLLOWUPS_CSV = ROOT / \"followups.csv\"\nOUT_HWPX = ROOT / \"renewal_playbook_updated.hwpx\"\n\nAPPENDIX_SENTENCE = \"이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.\"\n\nHP_NS = \"http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML\"\nNS = {\"hp\": HP_NS}\nET.register_namespace(\"hp\", HP_NS)\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef p_text(p_elem: ET.Element) -> str:\n    # HWPML stores visible text in <hp:t> nodes (often multiple runs).\n    texts = []\n    for t in p_elem.findall(\".//hp:t\", NS):\n        if t.text:\n            texts.append(t.text)\n    return \"\".join(texts)\n\n\ndef find_first(elem: ET.Element, xpath: str) -> ET.Element | None:\n    found = elem.find(xpath, NS)\n    return found\n\n\ndef rewrite_paragraph_to_single_run(p_elem: ET.Element, new_text: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Rewrite a paragraph's visible text to `new_text` using a single run.\n\n    This intentionally keeps paragraph-level properties (id, paraPrIDRef), but\n    collapses run structure to a single <hp:run><hp:t> block.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove all existing runs, but keep a copy of the first run to preserve charPrIDRef.\n    runs = list(p_elem.findall(\"hp:run\", NS))\n\n    if runs:\n        first_run = runs[0]\n        # Remove other runs\n        for r in runs[1:]:\n            p_elem.remove(r)\n    else:\n        first_run = ET.Element(f\"{{{HP_NS}}}run\")\n        p_elem.insert(0, first_run)\n\n    # Ensure exactly one <hp:t> under the first run\n    t_elems = list(first_run.findall(\"hp:t\", NS))\n    if t_elems:\n        t0 = t_elems[0]\n        # Remove additional <hp:t>\n        for t in t_elems[1:]:\n            first_run.remove(t)\n    else:\n        t0 = ET.SubElement(first_run, f\"{{{HP_NS}}}t\")\n\n    t0.text = new_text\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p_elem: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove <hp:linesegarray> / <hp:lineSegArray> children directly under <hp:p>.\"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n    for child in list(p_elem):\n        ln = localname(child.tag)\n        if ln in (\"linesegarray\", \"lineSegArray\"):\n            p_elem.remove(child)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef load_update_data() -> dict:\n    with UPDATE_JSON.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    required = [\n        \"customer\",\n        \"owner_name\",\n        \"window_start\",\n        \"window_end\",\n        \"band\",\n        \"escalation_email\",\n        \"note\",\n    ]\n    missing = [k for k in required if k not in data]\n encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            if not row:\n                continue\n            if \"sequence\" not in row or \"item\" not in row:\n                raise SystemExit(\"followups.csv must have columns: sequence,item\")\n            rows.append({\"sequence\": int(row[\"sequence\"]), \"item\": row[\"item\"].strip()})\n\n sorted(extracted_root.rglob(\"*.xml\"))\n\n\ndef identify_old_values(xml_trees: dict[Path, ET.ElementTree]) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Discover the \"old\" values currently present in the document.\"\"\"\n    old: dict[str, str] = {}\n\n    # Find by semantic labels; this avoids depending on exact run splitting.\n    def scan_for_label(label: str) -> tuple[ET.Element, str] | None:\n        for _, tree in xml_trees.items():\n            root = tree.getroot()\n            for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n                txt = p_text(p)\n                if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n                    continue\n                if label in txt:\n                    tail = txt.split(label, 1)[1].strip()\n                    return p, tail\n        return None\n\n    # 메모:\"),\n    ]:\n        found = scan_for_label(label)\n        if not found:\n            raise SystemExit(f\"Could not locate paragraph with label: {label}\")\n        _, tail = found\n        old[label_key] = tail\n\n    # Renewal window appears as two labels in this sample\n    found_window = scan_for_label(\"갱신 윈도우:\")\n    if not found_window:\n        raise SystemExit(\"Could not locate paragraph with label: 갱신 윈도우:\")\n    old[\"window\"] = found_window[1]\n\n    # Pricing band: pick first paragraph that looks like a band token or matches known value\n    band_candidates = []\n    for _, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        for p in tree.getroot().findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n            txt = p_text(p).strip()\n            if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in txt:\n                continue\n            if txt == \"Standard-18M\" or re.fullmatch(r\"[A-Za-z]+-\\d+M\", txt):\n                band_candidates.append(txt)\n    if not band_candidates:\n        raise SystemExit(\"Could not locate an existing pricing band paragraph\")\n    # Prefer the exact known one if present\n    old[\"band\"] = \"Standard-18M\" if \"Standard-18M\" in band_candidates else band_candidates[0]\n\n    # Escalation email: any paragraph containing '@' and ending with example domain, prefer known one\n    email_candidates = []\n    for _, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        for p in (\n        \"ops-old@northwind.example\" if \"ops-old@northwind.example\" in email_candidates else email_candidates[0]\n    )\n\n    return old\n\n\ndef update_fields_and_followups(\n    xml_trees: Returns (modified_paragraph_ids, evidence_log).\"\"\"\n\n    evidence: list[str] = []\n    modified: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()  # (xml_path, p_id)\n\n    start = update[\"window_start\"]\n    end = update[\"window_end\"]\n    new_window = f\"{start} ~ {end}\"\n\n    # 1) Update all paragraphs based on semantic labels / known patterns.\n    for xml_path, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        root = tree.getroot()\n        for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n            old_txt = p_text(p)\n\n            if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in old_txt:\n                continue\n\n            new_txt = old_txt\n\n            if \"고객사:\" in old_txt:\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"고객사:\\s*.*$\", f\"고객사: {update['customer']}\", old_txt)\n            if \"현 담당자:\" in old_txt:\n                new_txt = re.sub(r\"현 담당자:\\s*.*$\", f\"현 담당자: {update['owner_name']}\", new_txt)\n            if \"가격 메모:\" in old_txt:\n                # If changed, rewrite and remove layout cache\n            if new_txt != old_txt:\n                rewrite_paragraph_to_single_run(p, new_txt)\n                removed = remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p)\n                mark_modified(xml_path, p)\n                evidence.append(f\"Updated {xml_path.name} p@id={p.get('id')} : '{old_txt}' -> '{new_txt}' (removed layout={removed})\")\n\n    # Find numeric max for id allocation\n    numeric_ids = [int(x) for x in existing_ids if x.isdigit()]\n    max_id = max(numeric_ids) if numeric_ids else 0\n\n    def alloc_id(i: int) -> str:\n        nonlocal max_id\n        if i < len(old_ids) and old_ids[i] and old_ids[i] not in existing_ids:\n            return old_ids[i]\n        # Prefer reusing removed numeric ids (safe) for the first three\n        if i < len(old_ids) and old_ids[i] and old_ids[i].isdigit():\n            return old_ids[i]\n        max_id += 1\n        while str(max_id) in existing_ids:\n            max_id += 1\n        return str(max_id)\n\n    new_items = [r[\"item\"] for r in followups]\n\n    new_paras: list[ET.Element] = []\n    for i, item in enumerate(new_items, start=1):\n        p_new = deepcopy(template)\n        # Assign id safely\n        pid = alloc_id(i - 1)\n        p_new.set(\"id\", pid)\n\n        # Set text like \"1. <item>\"\n        new_line = f\"{i}. {item}\"\n        rewrite_paragraph_to_single_run(p_new, new_line)\n\n        # Ensure any modified paragraphs have had layout cache removed (already done in edit paths).\n    return modified, evidence\n\n\ndef write_xml_trees(xml_trees: dict[Path, ET.ElementTree]) -> None:\n    for path, tree in xml_trees.items():\n        tree.write(path, encoding=\"UTF-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n\n\ndef rezip_hwpx(extracted_root: Path, out_zip: Path, original_members: list[str]) -> None:\n    # Ensure structure matches original\n    rel_files = []\n    for p in extracted_root.rglob(\"*\"):\n        if p.is_file():\n            rel_files.append(p.relative_to(extracted_root).as_posix())\n\n    rel_set = set(rel_files)\n    orig_set = set(original_members)\n    if rel_set != orig_set:\n        missing = sorted(orig_set - rel_set)\n        extra = sorted(rel_set - orig_set)\n        raise SystemExit(\n            \"File structure mismatch vs original archive. \"\n            f\"Missing: {missing} Extra: {extra}\"\n        )\n\n    # Step 2: Extract\n    work_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"hwpx_work_\"))\n    try:\n        extract_hwpx(IN_HWPX, work_dir)\n\n        # Step 3+4: Parse and identify old values\n        xml_paths = gather_xml_files(work_dir)\n        if not xml_paths:\n            print(\"No XML files found in extracted HWPX\", file=sys.stderr)\n            return 2\n\n        xml_trees = {p: parse_xml(p) for p in xml_paths}\n        old_values = identify_old_values(xml_trees)\n        print(\"Identified old values:\")\n        for k, v in old_values.items():\n            print(f\" - {k}: {v}\")\n\n        # Step 5+6: Apply updates\n        modified, evidence = update_fields_and_followups(xml_trees, update, followups)\n\n        # After modifications, ensure appendix sentence still present in its paragraph and unchanged.\n        appendix_ok = False\n        for _, tree in xml_trees.items():\n            for p in tree.getroot().findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n                if p_text(p) == APPENDIX_SENTENCE:\n                    appendix_ok = True\n                    break\n            if appendix_ok:\n                break\n        if not appendix_ok:\n            raise SystemExit(\"Appendix sentence paragraph was modified or not found\")\n\n        write_xml_trees(xml_trees)\n\n        #
